एस०प्रो० 4 56.- इसके साथ लगी प्रथम अनुसूची में निर्धारित प्रत्येक अधिनियम के उपबन्ध के अन्तर्गत जारी किये गये निदेशों के अनुसार , 
केन्द्रीय सरकार फिल्म सलाहकार बो ४ बम्बई की सिफारिशों पर विचार करने के बाद, एतद्द्वारा इसके साथ लगी द्वितीय अनुसूची के कालम 2 
में दी गई फिल्म को उसके सभी भाषाओं के रूपांतर सहित जिसका विवरण उसके सामने उक्त द्वीतीय अनुसूची के कालम 6 में दिया हुआ है

एसः प्रो . 458.-- दमके साथ लगी प्रथम अनुसूची में निर्धारित प्रत्येक अधिनियम के उपबन्ध के अन्तर्गत जारी किये गये 
निवेशों के अनुसार , मन्द्रीय सरकार , फिल्म मलाहकार बोर्ड , गम्बई की सिफारिशों पर विचार करने के बाद , एसद्वारा, इसके माष 
नगी द्वितीय गनमत्री के कालम 2 में दी गई फिल्म को उसके मभी भाषाओं के रूपान्तरों महिम जिमका विवरण उसके सामने

New Delhi, the 24th December 1971 
3 . 0 . 459.--.- Whereas it appears to the Central Govern 
ment that coal is likely to be obtained from the lands 
mentioned in the Schedule hereto annexed ; 


The plan of the area covered by this notification can 
be inspected at the Omce of the National Coal Develop 
ment Corporation Limited (Revenue Section ) , Darbha 
nga House , Ranchi or at the Office of the Collector , 
Dhenkanal (Orissa ) , or at the Office of the Coal Con 
troller , 1, Council House Street , Calcutta. 


Now , therefore , in exercise of the powers conferred 
by sub - section ( 1 ) of section 4 of the Coal Bearing 
Areas ( Acquisition and Development ) Act , 1957 (20 of 
1957) , the Central Government hereby gives notice of 
its Intention to prospect for coal therein , 


All persons interegted in the land covered by this 
notification shall deliver all maps, charts and other 
documents referred to in sub -section ( 7 ) of section 18 
of the said Act to the Revenue Oncer , National Coal 
Development Corporation Limited , Darbhanga House , 
Ranchi within 30 days from the date of publication in 
the Gazette of India of this notification .

नई दिल्ली , 24 दिसम्बर, 1971 
का० प्रा० 459 . -- यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार को ऐसा प्रतीत होता है कि इसमे जपाबद अनुसूची में वर्णित भूमि में से कोयला अधिप्राप्त 
होने की सम्भावना है । 

अतः अब , कोयला वाले क्षेत्र ( अर्जन और विकास ) अधिनियम , 1957 ( 1957 का 20 ) की धारा 4 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त 
मितमों का प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा उसमें कोयले के लिए पूर्वेक्षण करने के अपने प्राशय की सूचना देती है । 

इस अधिसूचना के अतर्गत आने वाले क्षेत्र के रेखांक का निरीक्षण , राष्ट्रीय कोयला विकास निगम लिमिटेड ( राजस्व अनुभाग ), दर 
भंगा हाउस , रांची के कार्यालय अथवा कलक्टर , धेनकनाल ( उड़ीसा ) के कार्यालय में प्रथवा कोयला नियंत्रक , 1, काउंसिल हाऊस स्ट्रीट , 
कल । के कार्यालय में किया जा सकता है । 

इस अधिसूचना के अन्तर्गत पाने वाली भूमि में हितबस मभी व्यक्ति , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 13 की उपधारा ( 7) में निदशित 
समस्त मानचित्र , पार्ट और अन्य दस्तावेज , इस अधिसूचना के प्रकाशन की तारीख से 90 दिन के भीतर राजस्व अधिकारी, राष्ट्रीय कोयला 
विकास निगम लिमिटेड , दरभंगा हाऊस , गंची को परिदत्ता करेंगे ।

सीमा वर्णन 
क - ख लाइन ग्राम मधुपुर ( अल्हादनगर ) और राकस को भागतः सामान्य सीमा से , ग्राम दामादपुर ( अल्हादनगर ) पौर राकस , 

दामादपुर ( प्रल्हादनगर ) तथा अप्नाताबर्नी की भागतः सामान्य सीमा से होकर गुजरती है । 
ख -ग लाइन ग्राम दामादपुर ( अल्हादनगर ) और लक्ष्मणपुर की भागतः सामान्य सीमा से होकर गुजरती है ।

for the acquisition of land required in connection 
with installation of wireless sets for Flood Forecast 
ing Centres all over the country .

का० प्रा० 465. - - कर्मचारी राज्य बीमा अधिनियम 1948 ( 1948 का 34 ) की धारा 73च के द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग 
करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार इससे उपाबद्ध अनसूची के स्तंभ 4 में विनिर्दिष्ट कारखानों की उक्त प्रनसूची के स्तंभ 3 में विनिर्दिष्ट पश्चिमी 
बंगाल राज्य के ऐसे क्षेत्रों में जिसमें उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 4 और 5 के उपबंध प्रवृत्त नहीं हैं अवस्थिति को ध्यान में रखते 
हए उक्त कारखानों को उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 52 के अधीन उद्ग्रहणीय नियोजक के विशेष अभिदाय के संदाय से इस अधिसूचना के 
भारत के राजपत्र में प्रकाशन की तारीख से एक वर्ष की अवधि के लिए या तब तक के लिए जब नक कि उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 
5 के उपबंध उस क्षेत्र में प्रवृत्त नहीं हो जाते हैं , जो भी पहले हो , एतद्वारा छूट देती है ।

हए भारत सरकार के भूतपूर्व श्रम , रोमगार और पुनर्वास मंत्रालय ( श्रम और रोजगार विभाग ) की अधिसूचना संख्या का० मा० 
2977 तारीख 17 जलाई , 1971 के क्रम में केन्द्रीय सरकार इससे उपाबद्ध अन सूची के स्तंभ 4 में विनिर्दिष्ट कारखानों की उक 
अनमची के भ 3 में विनिर्दिष्ट अघ्रि प्रदेश राज्य के ऐसे क्षेत्रों में जिसमें उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 4 और 5 के उपबंध प्रवत्त नहीं 
हैं , अवस्थिति को ध्यान में रखते हुए , उक्त कारखाने को उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 5क के अधीन उदग्रहणीय नियोजक के विशेष अभि 
दाय के संदाय से उक्त अधिसूचना में विनिर्दिष्ट अवधि की समाप्ति की तारीख से एक वर्ष की और अवधि के लिए या मब तक के लिए 
जब तक कि उन अधिनियम के अध्याय 5 के उपबंध उन क्षेत्रों में प्रवृत्त नहीं हो जाते, जो भी पहले हो , एवारा छूट देती है ।

satisfied that Messrs Jyoti Electric Motors Limited was 
situated in Mogar area which was a sparse area (that

500 ) in the district of Kaira in the State of Gujarat ;

area the aforesaid factory was granted exemption from 
the payment of the employer s special , contribution 
under section 73F of the Employees State Insurance 
Act, 1948 ( 34 of 1948 ) , until enforcement of the provi 
sions of Chapter V of the Act in that area by the 
Central Government in the notification of the Govern 
ment of India in the Ministry of Labour and Rehabili 
tation (Department of Labour and Employment ) No. 
S . O . 2978 dated the 17th July , 1971 ; 


And , whereas the Central Government is satisfied 
that the insurable population of the Mogar area in the 
district of Kaira in the State of Gujarat has now 
exceeded 500 , and it is no longer a sparse area ; 


Now , therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred 
by section 73F of the Employees State Insurance Act , 
1948 ( 34 of 1948 ) , the Central Government hereby 
makes the following further amendment in the said 
notification, namely :

उपाबर अनुसूची के स्तंभ ( 4 ) में विनिर्दिष्ट कारखानों की उक्त 
अनुसूची के स्तंभ ( 3 ) में विनिर्विष्ट तामिल नाडु राज्य के ऐसे 
क्षेत्रों में , जिसमें उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 4 और 5 के उपबंध 


प्रवृत्त नहीं हैं , अवस्थिति को ध्यान में रखते हुए उक्स कारखाने को 
उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 5-क के अधीन उद्ग्रहणीय नियोजक के 
विशेष अभिदाय के संदाय से , 14 मार्च, 1972 से 13 मार्च, 1973 
तक जिसमें वह दिन भी सम्मिलित है एक वर्ष की अवधि के लिए 
या तब तक के लिए जब तक कि उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 5 के 
उपबंध उन क्षेत्रों में प्रवृत्त नहीं हो जाते, जो भी पहले हो , एतद्द्वारा 
छूट देती है ।

का० प्रा० 513.-- - यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार ने , भारत सरकार के 
श्रम और पुनर्वास मन्त्रालय ( श्रम और रोजगार विभाग ) की अधि 
सूचना सं० का० प्रा० 2710 तारीख 23 जून , 1971 द्वारा कोयला 
उद्योग को प्रोद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 का 14 ) 
के प्रयोजनों के लिए 8 जुलाई, 1971 से छः मास की और कालावधि 
के लिए लोक उपयोगी सेवा घोषित किया था ।

S . O . 517 . — Whereas the Central Government, having 
been satisfied that tlie public interest so required had , 
in pursuance of the provisions of sub - clause ( vi) of 
clause ( n ) of section 2 of the Industrial Disputes Act , 
1947 ( 14 of 1947) declared by the notification of the 
Government of India in the Ministry of Labour and 
Rehabilitation (Department of Labour and Employment) 
No . S . O . 3411 , dated the 4th August , 1971, service in the 
pyrites mining industry , to be a public utility service 
for the purposes of the said Act , for a period of six 
months from the 22nd August, 1971; 

And whereas the Central Government is of opinion 
that public interest requires extension of the said period 
by a further period of six months; 

Now , therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred 
by the proviso to sub- clause ( vi ) of clause ( n ) of Bection 
2 of the Industrial Disputes Act , 1947 (14 of 1947), the 
Central Government hereby declares the said service to 
be a public utility service for the purposes of the said 
Act for a further period of six months from the 22nd 
February , 1972.

एस० प्रो० 519 : - ~ यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि भारत के खाद्य निगम के भंडार- अभिकर्तामों , इससे उपाबद्ध 
अनुसूची के स्तम्भ 1 में उल्लिखित और उक्त अनुसूची के स्तम्भ 2 में की तत्स्थानी प्रविष्टियों में उल्लिखित कर्मकारों के बीच , उसके 
स्तम्भ 3 में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है : - - 


और यत :, केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिये निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है , 

अतः, अब , प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1917 का 14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) वारा प्रदत्त 
शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हो , केन्द्रीय सरकार तदद्वारा उक्त विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7 - 2 के अधीन गठित प्रौद्योगिक प्रधि 
करण सं० 2, धनबाद, को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिये निर्देशित करती है ।

का० प्रा० 521 - - यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि 
इससे उपाबद्ध अनसूची में विनिदि ट विषयों के बारे में सेंट्रल 
बैंक आफ इंडिया के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके 
कर्मकारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ; 


New Delhi, the 17th December 1971 
S . O . 520 . — Whereas a vacancy has occurred in the 
office of the presiding officer of the Labour Court with 
headquarters at Bangalore , constituted by the notifica 
tion of the Government of India in the Ministry ot 
Labour and Employment , No. S . O . 458 , dated the 5th 
February , 1963 ; 

Now , therefore , in pursuance of the provisions of 
section 8 of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 ( 14 of 
1947 ) , the Central Government hereby appoints Shri 
P , Srinivasacharya as the presiding officer of the Labour 
Court constituted as aforesaid . 

[ No. F .S . 11025 / 46 / 71 -LR.I. ] 
नई दिल्ली , 17 दिसम्बर 1971 
का० प्रा० 520 - - यत : भारत सरकार के श्रम और 
रोजगार मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना सं० का० प्रा० 459, तारीख 
5 फरवरी, 1963 द्वारा गठित श्रम न्यायालय , जिसका मुख्यालय , 
बंगलोर है , के पीठासीन अधिकारी का पद रिक्त हो गया है, 

अतः अब, प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 8 के उपबंधों के अनुसरण में , केन्द्रीय सरकार 
एतदद्वारा श्री पी . सिरीनिवासाचार्य को उपयुक्त रूप में 
गठित श्रम न्यायालय का पीठासीन अधिकारी नियुक्त करती है । 

[ सं० फा० एस० 11025/ 46/ 71- एल० प्रार० 1] 

New Delhi, the 20th December 1971 
S . O . 521 . Whereas the Central Government is of 
opinion that an industrial dispute exists Letween the 
employers in relation to the Central Bank of India and 
their workmen in respect of the matter specified in the 
Schedule hereto annexed ;

का० प्रा० 52 3. .. मुम्बई डा । कर्मकार (नियोजन का विनियमन ) 
स्कीम 1956 में और आगे संशोधन करने के लिये एक स्कीम का 
निम्नलिखित प्रारूप जिसे केन्द्रीय सरकार डाक कर्मकार योजन 
का विनियमन ) अधिनियम , 1948 ( 1948 का 9 ) की धारा 4 
की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए बनाने 
की प्रस्थापना करती है उम के द्वारा संभाव्यन : प्रभावित होने 
वाले सभी व्यक्तियों की जानकारी के लिये उक्त धारा द्वारा यथा 
अपेक्षित प्रकाशित किया जाता है, और एतद्द्वारा सूचना दी जाती 
है कि उक्त प्रारूप पर 17 फरवरी, 1972 को या उस के पश्चात 
विचार किया जाएगा । 


इम प्रारूप के बारे में किसी भी व्यक्ति से यथाविनिदिष्ट 
नारीख के पूर्व प्राप्त हुए आक्षेपों या सुझावों पर केन्द्रीय सरकार 
द्वारा विचार किया जाएगा ।

विनियमन ) संशोधन स्कीम , 1971 होगा ।

( iii ) कोई कर्मकार किसी भी अवधि या अवधियों 

के लिए जिस में कर्मकार के नियंत्रण के परे

होगा ।

या कर्मकारों की टोली जो एक मास में कार्य करने

यमों को बनाने में असफल रहे तो केन्द्रीय सरकार

अंगीकृल करे और बोई इस प्रकार दिए गए 

निवेशों को कार्यान्वित करने को बाध्य होगा ।

ment on the 14th January , 1972. 


They also notified in the same notice that such of the 
workmen concerned as are entitled to compensation 
under Section 25C of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 
will be paid compensation due to them provided they 
get themselves marked present by calling on their

Section personally in between 9. 00 A . M . to 9 . 30 A. M . On

3 . Immediately on receipt of the lay -off notice, the 
General Secretary uf Gua Dock Labour Union repre 
senting the workmen wrote to the Project Manager 
of M / s . Ivan Milutinovic - Pim stating that the reasons 
for the lay -off stated in the Annexure to the lay -ott 
notice do not justify the action by the management 
in laying off 481 workmen , apart from the contents 
being vague in respect of compensation . In that let 
ter, he further stated that if the management s con 
tentions are correct, mistead of supping the work and 
laying off the workmen , a settlement could have been 
arrived at with the Mormugao Port Trust failing which 
the matter could have been raised in arbitration and 
secured compensation by substantiating their conten 
tions and that in the circumstances , laying off the 
workmen was not justified . As a result of discus 
sions held by the Assistant Labour Commissioner 
(Central), Vascodagama, both the empluyer s and the 
union agreed for the extension of lay - off up to 15 - 9 - 71 
and subsequently up to 24th September , 1971 In view 
of the pending negotiations between the contractors 
and their principals at the higher level. Following 
further discussions between the parties with the as 
sistance of the A . L . C . ( C ) , Vascodagama, a memoran 
dum of settlement, dated 30 - 9 -71 was signed in which 
the parties agreed that all the laid - off workmen will 
be paid , besides the lay off compensation at 50 per 
cent of wages in terms of Section 25C of the Industrial 
Disputes Act, an ad hoc advance equivalent to 50 per 
cent of the wages for the period of lay -off from 1 - 9 - 71 
to 24 - 9 - 71. The management also agreed, as a mat 
ter of goodwill, that they will pay the lay -off com 
pensation and the ad hoc advance even to workmen 
who have put in less than one year s continuous ser 
vice. The parties also agrecd that the dispute regard 
ing justifiability of the lay - off and the demand for 
full payment of wages for the lay -off period will be 
referred to the arbitration of Shri O . Maheepathi, 
Deputy Chief Labour Commissioner (Central) , New 
Delhi, under Section 10A of the Industrial Disputes 
Act, 1947 . 


denied that the lay -off was as a result of financial loss 
and stated that in fact they had raised the grades of 
their workmen effective from January 1971 wuch are 
considered to be che best in Goa and had also declared 
10 per cent annual bonus to their worlamen for the 
calendar year of 1970 . They reiterated that it was 
only due to the circumstances beyond their control 
which compelled them to take this action of laying off 
the workmen even though they would have been justi 
fied in terminating the services of the workinen , They 
quoted the provisions of their certified Standing Or 
ders which permit laying off of workmen und treat 
lag the period of unemployment as compulsory leave 
either with or without pay . They contended that 
" the words any other reason must be construed to 
mean some reason analogous to the reasons specified 
in the Section and must be integrally in connection 
with production , that the several circumstances men 
tioned in the definition are dillerent species of the same 
genius and the distinguishing features of that genus are 
those circumstances which rendered the employer un 
able to give employment i.e . beyond the control of the 
einployer and from this point of view variance in phy 
sical conditions quite fits the definition in Section 
2 ( kk ) of the Industrial Disputes Act, as well as in 
the wordings of the certified Standing Orders ." They 
also mentioned that the management had agreed to pay 
the laid - off workmen including those who have put in 
Cess than one year s continuous service an ad hoc ad 
vance equivalent to 50 per cent of the wages for the 
period of lay off from 1 - 4 - 71 to 24 - 9 - 71 and they in 
formed the workmen on 24 - 9 - 71 that the lay - off was 
lifted w . e . f. 25 - 4 - 71 though the work had not been re 
sumed by then , and the actual work was started from 
5 - 11 - 71, and as usual the management was generous 
enough not to continue the lay -off or to retrench the 
workmen , 

5 . On the other hand , the union contended that the 
lay - off was illegal and unilateral and as such the work 
men are entitled to full wages for the period from 1 - 9 
71 to 24 - 9 - 71 during which period they were illegally 
laid -oil . They stated that the compelling reasons for 
the lay - off viz . physical connitions of the project found 
at variance with the estimated conditions could have 
been sorted out by invoking the arbitration clause in 
their contract to iron out their differences . They also 
stated that lay - off can take place only when the em 
ployer is unable to give employment to the workmen on 
account oť shortage of coal or power or raw materials 
or accumulation of stocks or breakdown of machinery 
or for any other reason and that the reason stated by 
the management for lay - off does not come under the 
list of reasons specified in the definition of " lay - off" in 
the Industrial Disputes Act . In support of their con 
tentions, they crusa Supreme Court decisions in Dewan 
Tea Estate case ( 1964 1 LIJ 358 ) and also in Karrabet 
ta Estate case ( 1960 I LLJ 275 ) . They therefore press 
ed that the lay - off was in contravention of Section 
2 ( kkk ) of the Industrial Disputes Act and that it was 
illegal and an illegal act cannot be jusiiled and hence 
pleaded that the management be directed to pay full 
wages to the workmen laid -ofl. 

6 . The definition of " lay - off" as given in Section 
2 (kkk ) of the Industrial Disputes Aci, 1947 is as fol 
lows: --- 
" (kkk ) " lay -off " (with its grammatical variations 

and cognate expressions ) means the failure, re 
fusal or inability of an employer on account 
of shortage of coal, power or raw materials or 
the accumulation of stocks or the breakdown 
of machinery or for any other reason to give 
employment to a workman whose name is 
borne on the muster rolls of his industrial es 
tablishment and who has not been retrench 
ed ;" . 


4 . The main contention of the employers was that 
the data supplied to them was incorrect and mislcad 
ing about the nature of soil and silt and the materials 
which were available for reclamation , that they wrote 
to the authoricies that they did not wish to continue 
the work and treat the agreement as cancelled , and 
that they would have been justified in closing down 
the establishment but thcy took a course which is not 
80 arduous and difficult to the labour by laying off 
the workmen and in fact it would have been morc 
economical for them to pack themselves for good in 
stead of continuing on false hopes. They also stated 
that though their day - to - day Anancial commitments 
were heavy without any production , they did not wish 
to put their workmen to any financial hardship though 
they were within their rights to lay them off accord 
ing to the provisions of the Industrial Disputes Act . 
They quoted the definition of " lay -off" in that Act 
and relied on the fact that lay off could be for any 
other reason to give employment to the workmen . 
They further stated that though they were perfectly 
within their rights to terminate the services of all the 
workmen after meeting all the obligations under the 
law , they refrained from taking these steps as they 
thought that something better may come out from the 
discussions to tide over the difficulties which are of 
physical and technical nature . They also denied that 
the lay - off was sudden and unilateral and stated that 
the union s contention that the lay - off was illegal and 
unilateral and as such the workmen are entitled to 
get full wages for the period of lay -off from 1 - 9 -71 to 
24 - 9 -71 was baseless and without foundation . They

or revision . The employer is also at liberty to have 
closed down his establishment if he had chosen to da 
90 . Further , the provisions of the contract betwcen 
Morm1780 Port Trust an ! M / s. Ivan Tiutinovic - Pim 
provide for a procedure including that of arbitration for 
gettlement of differencne and lisafrrements . The con

their workinen under their Standing Orders without 
vages cannot hold good as any provisions of the 
Standine Orders which are in contravention of any 
statute cannot be enforced . 

7. I have given careful consideration to the fucts of

I have rome to thn conclusion that the lay -off resorted

from 1 - 9 - 71 to 24 - 9 - 71 does not come strictly within

4 . On 28th October , 1971 Shri B . Lal appearing on 
behalf of the workman Aled an application stating that 
as the management of the colliery concern had been 
taken over by the Central Government, the Custodian 
General and the Custodian should be impleaded as par . 
ties to the proceedings. Notices were issued to the Cus 
todian General and the Custodian to show cause by 18th 
November 1971, why they should not be added as parties 
to the proceeding . Two written statements in identical 
language were Aled on 10th January, 1972 by Shri J. N . P . 
Sahl, one on behalf of the Custodian General and another 
on behalf of the Custodian . It may be noted that the 
hearing on the preliminary point took place on 15th 
October, 1971 in the absence of the Custodian General 
and the Custodian because the application for adding 
ther parties was Aled subsequent to that date . 13th 
January, 1972 was fxed for passing an order on the 
preliminary point. 

5 . I propose to consider the evidence and arguments 
on the preliminary point in order to give my decision 
on the preliminary point. Shri Bhola Gope the worlc 
man concerned was appointed on 1st December, 1965 
as A Night Guard or Depot Guard in the Busserya 
Colliery . A chargeshcet dated 17th July , 1970 was 
Issued to Shri Bhola Gope for riotous and disorderly 
hehaviour. for disobedience of lawful order and also 
for remaining absent from duty without leave. Phola 
Gope submitted his reply dated 26th August , 1970 tn 
the above chargesheet, and the said reply was received 
by the employers on 3rd September , 1970 

8 . Another chargeshee ! dated 20th August, 1970 was 
given to him on the allegation that he indulged in 
further riotous and disorderly behaviour on 17131 
August , 1970 Shri Bhola Gope submitted his reply dlated 
26th August 1970 to the above chargesheet anri the sald 
was received by the management on 3rd September , 
1970 , 

7 . Two separate domestic enquiries were held into the 
two chargesheets resprctively dated 17th July , 1970 and 
20th August, 1970 . It has been alleged in paragraph 
11 of the written statement submitted by the emplovers 
that in spite of repeated chances given to Shri Bholr 
Gope , he refused to attend the domestic enquiries and 
as such they had to be held in his absence 

8 . In paragraph 12 of the said written statement it 
has been stated that in the departmental enquiries 
misconducts mentioned in the chargesheets nated 17 ! h 
July , 1970 and 20th August , 1970 were satisfactorily 
established and that Shri Bhola Gope was therefore 
dismissed by a letter dated 11th December , 1970 by live 
Director of the company after he was satisfied that the 
misconducts against Shri Bhola Gope had been satis 
factorily established . It has also hann stated in para 
graph 13 that the past records of Shri Bhola Gope were 
also not satisfactory and that he was suspender or 
warned several times in the past for proved miscon 
ducts . 

9 . The workmen have made serious allegations again 1 
the managoment in their written statement dated 2nd 
June, 1971. They have stated in paragraphs 3 and 4 that 
in 1969 workmen of this colliery enrolled themselves 
as members of the Kuyala Mazdoor Panchayal, that they 
started some agitation for their genuine grievances , 
that Bhola Gope took a leading part in this agitation 
which culminated in a criminal proce - ding U / S 107 
Cr. P . C . against some of his co -workers . It has further 
been stated that while Bhola Gode was working in 
the office he was transferred on 15th February , 1970 to 
work as a Night Guard at the back side of the Director s 
Bungalow and that he accepted the said order and 
started his work at that place . 


11 . It has been alleged in paragraph 8 of the written 
statement that Bhola Gope fell ill with effect from 10th 
July , 1970 and was under the treatment of Dr . M . P . 
Singh , Civil Assistant Surgeon , Sadar Hospital at 
Dhanbad and that he sent an application with medical 
certificate to the Manager of the Colliery requesting 
for grant of sick leave. It has further been alleged in 
paragraph 9 that on receipt of the transfer order on 
16th July , 1970 Bhola Gope went to the office of the 
Manager to hand over the medical certifcate and appli 
cation again with a prayer to grant him sick leave and 
that at the interview he also requested that his duty 
place should be changed from No. 3 siding to any other 
suitable place. 

12. Their further case is that this simple request was 
concocted as a threat to the Personnel Officer , that on 
the same day namely 10th July , 1970 a report was made 
to the Police alleging apprehension of breach of peace 
and that Bhola Gope s co -workers were implicated in a 
criminal proceeding U / S 107 Cr. P . C . 

13 . According to the workmen , Bhola Gope remained 
sick under the treatment of Dr. M . P . Singh , Civil 
Assistant Surgeon , Sadar Hospital, Dhanbad till 9th 
August , 1970 when he was declared At to join his duties 
but he was not allowed to join his duty on 10th August, 
1970 after his recovery from illness . They have further 
stated that by a letter dated 12th August, 1970 received 
by Bhola Cope on 14th August, 1970 he was asked to 
give his reply to the chargesheet dated 17th July , 1970 . 

14 . They admit that by a letter dated 20th August , 
1970 another allegation för riotous hehaviour and use 
of Althy language was made against Bhola Gope by 
the Manager . They have also given the gist of the 
allegation in paragraph 14 of their written statement. 

15 . On the question of departmental enquiry the ver 
sion of the workmen is as follows. A date of enguiry 
into the allegations was Axed for 21st October , 1970 . 
Bhola Gope went to the office of the Manager , the place 
appointed for the enguiry hut there was none to hold 
the enquiry and he reported to the Manager in writing 
and also forwarded a copy of the same to the Assistant 
Labour Commissioner ( C ) , Dhanbad for information . 
He received two letters hoth dated 26th October , 1970 
on 7th November, 1970 by registered post in which It 
was stated that enquiries would be held in the two 
chargesheets on 4th November , 1970 and 7th November , 
1970 . 

16 . They admit that the next dates for the two 
enquiries were Axed on 8th December , 1970 and 9th 
December, 1970. Their complaint is that the name of 
the Enquiry Officer was never intimated to Bhola Gope. 
Their further complaint is that though Bhola Gope 
went to the place of enquiry, there was none to hold 
the enquiry and that he reported the same to the 
Manager before leaving the place of enquiry . They 
have characterised the ex -parte enquiries that took 
place as inerely a show . Their grievance is that Bhola 
Gope was denied the opportunity of listening to the wit 
nesses on behalf of the prosecution and put his defence 
witnesses . 

17. They have stated in paragraph 21 that as the 
copies of statements recorded on behalf of the prosecu 
tion were nelther handed over nor sent to Bhola Gope 
ly registered post , he could not produce his witnesses 
before the Enquiry Ofcer . They have also cumplained 
that no date was fixed for the production of defence 
witnesses to counter the statements of the prosecution 
witnesses . The enquiry proceedings, according to them , 
are vitiated , biased and perverse. They assert that as 
natural Justice has been denied to Bhola Gope the pro 
ceedings should be quashed . 

18 . Let me now consider the evidence for the pur 
pose of ascertaining whether the two enquiries were 
properly held or not and whether the management was 
justified in dismissing him on the two reports gub . 
mitted by the Enquiry Officer in respect of the two

area.

of lawful order and also for remaining abuent from 
duly without any authorised leave. It is very dimncuir 
to congtrue Ext. Mo as a chargesheet. Only a threat 
was held out to him that disciplinary action would be 
taken for his riotous and disobedience behaviour, 
Nevertheless he was asked to join hie duty at No. 3 
siding 

20 . Shri Bhola Gope sent a l eply to this alleged 
chargesheet which was received by the management 
on 23rd Juiy , 1970 . He stuted in the said letter that 
his state or health did not permit him to work at No. 8 
$ iding the pointed out that he would be seriously 
prejudiced If some light work was not allotted to him 
as he was still very very weak . He assured that he 
would work at No , 3 siding when his health would per 
mit nim to do so . The Manager sent a reply to Bnola 
Gope by his letter dated 26th July , 1970 . The subject 
matter of the said letter was as follows: " Change of 
place of work and duties with eilect from yth July , 1970 
to work as Depot Guard at No . 3 siding ." There was a 
reference in that letter to the two letters dated 16th 
July , 1970 and 21st July , 1970 written by Shri Bhola 
Gope to the management. The Manager denied that 
he had received any letter dated 10th July , 1970 or 
any oral information about the ill-health of Bhola Gope . 
He further pointed out that he had not till then rece 
Ived medical certificate in support of the alleged illness 
of Bhola Gope. He also pointed out that he had not 
received any leave application . He expressed his in 
ability to find any reason why Shri Bhola Gope should 
not be able to work at No. 3 siding when he was ready 
and fit to work at No. 2 and 4 sidingo or 39 incline. 


You may join your duty at No. 3 siding and submit

Manager," 


23 . In this letter also the Manager asked him to 
join duty at No. 3 siding and submit satisfactory 
explanation to the chargesheet dated 17th July , 1970 
within 48 hours from the receipt of the letter . 


24 . This lengthy correspondence between the parties 
even after the submission of the chargesheet indicates 
that the management had no intention of treating the 
letter dated 17th July , 1970 as a chargesheet. Even if 
it is to be regarded as a chargęsheet , it was conditional 
in nature. He was threatened with action if he failed 
to join his duty at No. 3 siding. I am therefore not 
inclined to attach much importance to the enquiry 
proceedings that took place on the basis of this charge 
sheet.

3 . Sri S . A . Aktar is present on behaf of the manage 
ment and Sri R . Sen is present for the workmen 
Heard both the representatives. Considered each of 
the items of Clause ( i ) , sub - clauses ( a ) , ( b ) , ( c ) and 
( d ) of the compromise petition . Sub - clause ( a ) is 
clearly for the benefit of the workman , His pay would 
be increased by 37 paise per day with immediate effect 
1.e , 11th November 1971 . The worker , by sub - clause 
( b ) , would get after 3 months from the date of the 
compromise , provided his work is found satisfactory , a 
lift to the post of Loading Supervisor and would be 
graded As a monthly paid staff with a starting basic 
pay of Rs. 180 per month . This clause is also very 
much beneficial to the workman concerned . By sub 
clauses ( c ) and ( d ) the Union shall have no claim 
whatsoever in relation to the dispute and the dispute 
is fully and finally settled between the parties , 

4 . Considering each and every clauses of the settle 
ment and hearing the representatives of the parties , I 
And that the compromise is just, fair and equitable and 
resolves the dispute between the parties. According 
ly , I record the compromise and pass an award in terms 
of the compromise field before me to which neither 
party objects . Let the petition of compromise form 
part of this award . 

( Sd. ) S . N . BAGCHI , 

Presiding Officer,

का० प्रा० 534 ~ - यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि 
इससे उपावर अनसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में सिंगरेनी 
कोलियरीक कम्पनी लिमिटड की रामागडम प्रभाग 1 से सम्बद्ध 
नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद 
विद्यमान है : 


और यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के 
लिए निशित करना वांछनीय सममती है ;

का० प्रा० 533 . ----यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि 
इससे उपाबद्ध अनसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में सिंगरेनी 
कोलियरीज कम्पनी लिमिटड , डाकघर कोठागडियम कोलियरीज 
( प्रान्ध्र प्रदेश ) के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों 
के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ; 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के 
लिए निर्देशित करता बांछनीय समझती है ; 

अतः, अब, प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 7-क और धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड 
( घ ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए , केन्द्रीय सरकार 
एतद्वारा एक प्रौद्योगिक अधिकरण गठित करती है जिसके 
पीठासीन अधिकारी श्री पी० एस० अानन्थ होंगे, जिनका मुख्यालय 
अफजल लॉज , तिलक रोड, रामकोट , हैदराबाद- 1 होगा और 
उक्त विवाद को उक्त प्रौद्योगिक अधिकरण को न्यायनिर्णयन के 
लिए निर्देशित करती है ।

S . O . 510 . - WHEREAS the industrial disputes applications and complaints respectively specified in Schedule - 1, Schedule - II 
and Schedule - III hereto annexed ( hereinafter referred to as the said disputes , applications and complaints ) were pending before 
Shri T . Chandrasekhara Reddy , Presiding Officer , Central Industrial Tribunal with headquartersatHyderabad ; 

AND WHEREAS Shri T .Chandrasekhara Reddy s ,services have ceased to be available ; 

NOW , THEREFORE , in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 7A and sub - sectior ( 1 ) of sectick 33B of the Industrial 
Disputes Act, 1947 ( 14 of 1947 ), the CentralGovernment hereby constitutes an Industrial Tribur al with Shri P . S . Ananth as the 
Presiding Officer with headquarters at Hyderabad , withdraws the proceedings in relation to the said disputes and the applica. 
tions under the provison to sub - section ( 2 ) of section 33 and complaints under section 33A ct the said Act ard arisirg out the 
said disputes, applications and complaints from Shri T . Chandrasekhara Reddy and transfers the same to Shri P . S . Ananth , 
Presiding Officer , Industrial Tribunal,Hyderabad , for thedisposal of the said disputes, applications and complaints with the 
direction that the said Tribunal shall proceed with the proceedings from the stage at which they are transferred to it and 
dispose of the same according to law .

पत्र मार शिकायतें (जिन्हें इसके बाद उक्त विवाद प्रावेदन पत्र और शिकायतों के रूप में निर्दिष्ट किया गया है ), श्री टी० चन्द्रशेखर रेड्डी , 
पीठासीन अधिकारी, केन्द्रीय प्रौद्योगिक प्रधिकरण, जिनका मुख्यालय हैदराबाद में है, के समक्ष लम्बित थें ; 

और यतः श्री टी० चन्द्रशेखर रेड्डी की सेवाएं अब उपलब्ध नहीं रही हैं ;

अतः, अब , प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 का 14 ) की धारा 7 -क और धारा 33 - ख की उपधारा (i ) द्वारा प्रदत्त 
शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतदद्वारा एक प्रौद्योगिक प्राधिकरण गठित करती है जिसके पो ठासीन अधिकारी श्री पी० एस०अानन्य 
होंगे, जिनका मुख्यालय हैदराबाद होगा, श्री टी० चन्द्रशेखर रेड्डी से उक्त वि । दो और आवेदन पत्रों से सम्बन्ध कार्यवाहियों को उक्त अधिनियम 
की धारा 33 की उपधारा ( 2 ) के परन्तुक के अधीन और शिकायतों से सम्बन्ध कार्यवाहियों को धारा 33 के अधीन वापस लेती है और उन्हें श्री 
पी० एस० अनन्थ, पीठासीन अधिकारी, प्रौद्योगिक प्रधिकरण, हैदराबाद को उक्त विवादों, प्रावेदन पत्रों और शिकायतों के निपटान के लिए 
निदेश के साथ स्थानान्तरित करती है कि उक्त अधिकरण और पागे कार्यवाहियां उसी प्रक्रम से करेगा जिस पर वे उसे स्थानान्तरित की जाएं 
और विधि के अनुसार उनका निपटार करेगा ।

10, एम० पी० सं० 24/ 71 


- - -- - - - -- -- -- - 


New Delhi, the 16th November 1971 
$ . 0 . 541, - Whereas the Central Government is of 
opinion that an industrial dispute exists between the 
employers in relation to the management of Lachipur 
Colliery of Messrs Katras Jharia Coal Company Limit 
ed , Post Omce Kajoragram , District Burdwan and their 
workmen in respect of the matters specified in the 
Schedule hereto annexed ; 

And, whereas , the Central Government considers it 
desirable to refer the said dispute for adjudication ; 

Now , therefore , in exercise of the powers conferred 
by clause ( d ) of sub - section ( 1 ) of section 10 of the 
Industrial Disputes Act , 1947 ( 14 of 1947 ) , the Cen 
tral Government hereby refers the said dispute for 
adjudication to the Central Government Industrial 
Tribunal, Calcutta , constituted under section 7A of the 
sald Act. 

SCHEDULE 
" Whether the action of the management of Lachipur 
Colliery of Messrs Katras Jharia Coal Company Limit 
ed , Post Office Kajoragram , District Burdwan in stop 
ping the work of ( 1 ) Sarvashri Azad Khan , ( 2 ) Dine 
nath Shaw , ( 3 ) Md. Nasiruddin , (4 ) Dahara Turi, ( 5 ) 
Suleman Mla , Stone Cutters and ( 6 ) Isrilo Mia , ( 7 ) 
Maiuddin Mia , ( 8 ) Ambika Singh , (9 ) Saidagar Yadab , 
(10 ) Jainan Mia , (11) Fakir Chand , ( 12 ) Chandrika 
Yadab . (13 ) Mahabir Das, ( 14 ) Majo Bouri, ( 15 ) Moti 
Rajak , ( 10 ) Barhamdeo Modi, ( 17 ) Triloke Yadab , ( 18 ) 
Bhuneswar Yadav, Stone Cutter Mazdoor , with effect 
from the 4th August, 1970 is justified ? If not, what 
relief are the workmen entitled to ?" 

[ No. L/ 1912 / 80 / 71-LRII .] 
नई दिल्ली, 16 नवम्बर 1971 


[ संख्या एल0 / 2125 / 3/ 71 -एल०मार० - 2)

का०मा० 543. - यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि 
इससे उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में मैमर्स 
गोधुर कोलियरी कम्पनी की गोधुर कोलियरी; डाकघर कुसुंडा , 
जिला धनबाद के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों 
के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ; 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के 
लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 

अतः, अब , औद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा

के लिए निर्देशित करती है । 


और यत : केन्द्रीय मरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन 
के लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ।

का 14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( ध ) द्वारा

का० प्रा० 544.- ---यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इससे ! 
उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में दी सेन्द्रा बांसजोरा 
कोलियरी कम्पनी प्राइवेट लिमिटेड की सेन्द्रा बांसजोरा कोलियरी, 
गकपर सरिया जिला धनबाद के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और 
उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है । 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के 
सिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ।

घर यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए

का०मा० 549. --- यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इससे 
उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में मैसर्स इक्वीटेबल 
कोल कम्पनी लिमिटेड , के दिशेरगढ़ कार्यालय डाकचर दिशेरगढ 
जिला बर्दवान के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों 
के बीच एक औद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है । 


का० प्रा० 550. -~-पतः नेशनल मिनरल्स डेवेलपमेंट कारपो 
रेशन लिमिटड के डोनीमलाई पायरन और प्रोजेक्ट डाकघर सान्दुर 
के प्रबन्ध के सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों जिनका प्रतिनि 
धित्व डोनीमलाई आयरन और प्रोजेक्ट इम्प्लाईज ऐसोसिएशन सान्दुर 
करती है ने औद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम 1947 ( 1947 का 14 ) 
की धारा 10 की उप- धारा ( 2 ) के अधीन संयुक्त रूप से केन्द्रीय 
सरकार को आवेदन किया है कि वह उनके बीच विद्यमान प्रौद्यो 
गिक विवाद को उक्त आवेदन में उपणित और इससे उपाबर 
अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में किसी प्रौद्योगिक अधि 
करण को निर्देशित करें , 


और यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार का समाधान हो गया है कि 
आवेदन करने वाले व्यक्ति प्रत्येक पक्ष के बहुमत का प्रतिनिधित्व 
करते हैं ; 


और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्याय निर्णयन के 
लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 

अतः, अब , प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 का 
14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा प्रदत्त 
शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हए , केन्द्रीय सरकार एतदद्वारा उक्त 
विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7 -क के अधीन गठित केन्द्रीय 
सरकार पोयोगिक प्रधिकरण , कलकत्ता को न्याय निर्णयन के लिए 
निर्देशित करती है । 

अनुसूची 
" क्या मैसर्स इक्वीटेबल कोल कम्पनी लिमिटेड, डाकघर 
विशेरंगढ़, जिला बर्दवान के प्रबन्ध मण्डल की श्री दाहारी 
राउथ , रक्षक, दिशेरगढ़ कार्यालय को 31 जुलाई 
1971 से पदच्युत करने की कार्यवाही न्यायोचित 


प्रतः अब प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम 1947 ( 1947 
का 14) की धारा 7- क और धारा 10 की उपधारा 2 द्वारा 
प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुये केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्द्वारा 
एक औद्योगिक अधिकरण गठित करती है जिसके पीठासीन अधि 
कारी श्री पी० एस० प्रानन्थ होंगे जिनका मुख्यालय हैदराबाद 
होगा और उक्त विवाद को उक्त प्रौद्योगिक अधिकरण को न्याय 
निर्णयन के लिये निर्देशित करती है ।

THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : FEBRUARY 12, 1972 / MAGHA 23 , 1893 


[ PART 11 


अनुसूची

इससे उपाबद्ध अनूसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में रियल 
काजोरा कोलियरी, डाकघर काजोराग्राम, जिला बर्दवान के प्रबन्ध

विवाद विद्यमान है ।

का 14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा 
प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए; केन्द्रीय सरकार एसद्वारा 
उक्त विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7-क के अधीन गठित 
केन्द्रीय सरकार भौद्योगिक प्राधिकरण , कलकत्ता को न्यायनिर्णयन 
के लिए निर्देशित करती है ।

पन्न द्वारा उसमें निर्दिष्ट तारीख 15 सितम्बर , 1970 के

का प्रा० 553 . . . - - यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि 
इससे उपाबद अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में मैसर्स 
वैस्टर्न गाल कोल फील्ड्स लिमिटेड , बेतरी कापर प्रोजेक्ट , 
घेतरी नगर के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के 
बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है । 

और यत : कनीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए 
निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 

अतः, अम, औद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 7-क और धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड 
( घ ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए , केन्द्रीय सरकार 
एतदद्वारा एक प्रौद्योगिक प्रधिकरण गठित करती है जिसके 
पीठासीन अधिकारी श्री गोपाल नारायण शर्मा होंगे , जिनका मुस 
यालय जयपुर होगा और उक्त विवाद को उक्त प्रौद्योगिक प्रधिकरण 
को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए निदशित करती है ।

" क्या हड़ताल की अवधि 7 सितम्बर, 1971 से 25सितम्बर , 

1971 ( 4 बजे अपराहन ) तक समझी जानी चाहिए 
या 7 सितम्बर , 1971 से 27 सितम्बर , 1971 
( 9 बज पूर्वाहन ) तक और क्या हड़ताल की अवधि 
के लिए श्रमिकों को प्रबन्ध मण्डल द्वारा मजदूरी देय 
होनी चाहिए और अगर हां तो किस सीमा तक ? "

के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ।

है कि इससे उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में श्री

का० प्रा० 567. - -- यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि 
इससे उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में मैसर्स 

ओरिएन्टल कोल कम्पनी लिमिटेड की बाद्जना कोलियरी, डाक 
घर निरसा चट्टी, जिला धनबाद के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों 
और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ; 

और यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन 
के लिएनिशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 

अत :, अब, प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 
( 1947 का 14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) 
दयारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हए, केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद् 
देवारा उक्त विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7 - क के अधीन 
गठित केन्द्रीय सरकार प्रौद्योगिक प्रधिकरण ( संख्या 1 ) , धनबाद 
को न्य यनिर्णयन के लिए निर्देशित करती है । 


का० प्रा० 566. - --यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि 
इसने उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में मैसर्स गोधर 
कोलियरी कम्पनी की गोधुर कोलियरी, डाकघर कुसुन्डा , जिला 
धनबाद के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के 
बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ; 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन 
के लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 

अतः, अब , प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 
का 14) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खंड ( घ ) द्वारा 
प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए , केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्द्वारा 
उक्त विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7- क के अधीन गठित 
केन्द्रीय सरकार प्रौद्योगिक अधिकरण (संख्या 1 ) धनबाद 
को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए निर्देशित करती है ।

New Delhi, the 3rd February 1972 


It is hereby agreed between the above parties that 
in partial modification of the Memorandum of Settle 
ment dated 27th January, 1970 , made between the 
parties in respect of the Constitution of the Arbitrators, 
the following industrial disputes be referred to the sole 
arbitration of Shri Mohan Lal Sukhadla , former Chief 
Minister of Rajasthan , whose consent to arbitrate in 
the matters hereinafter mentioned has been obtained . 

I. Specific matters in dispute in respect of the 
Limestone Quarries at Phallodi and Bajrakho / Bheron 
pura on which arbitration is required : 
1. Whether Bonus for the accounting year 1964 - 65 

already paid by M / s . Jaipur Udyog Ltd ., 
Sawaimadhopur, @ 4 . 78 % as worked out under 
the Profit and Loss Accounts under Balance 
Sheet was in accordance with the Payment of 
Bonus Act, 1965 or not ? And whether work 
men , represented by he ( ement Works Karam 
chari Sangh , Sawaimadhopur, are entitled to 

any relleſ for the year in question , under law ? 
2 . Whether bonus for the accounting years 1965- 66 , 

1966 - 67 , 1967-68 and 1968 -69, already paid by 
M / s . Jaipur Udyog Limited , Sawaimadhopur, 
@ 4 per cent, 4 per cent, 5 per cent and 4 per 
cent respectively , according to the allocable 
surplus worked out under the Profit and Loss 
Accounts under Balance Sheet is in accordance 
with the Payment of Bonus Act , 1965 or not ? 
And whether workmen represented by the 
Cement Works Karamchari Sangh , Sawaima 
dhopur, are entitled to any relief for the yeart 

in question , under law ? 
3 . Whether the workmen in Quarries , whose names 

are mentioned in Annexure I have not been 
suitably fixed according to the Second Wage 
Board for Cement Industry and if so , to what 

relief are they entitled ? 
4 . Whether the workmen whose names are given 

in Annexure II and who were dismissed from 
the services on the charge of assault , are 

entitled to any relief ? If so , to what extent ? 
5 . Whether the demand to have the same age of 

superannuation of workmen in Phallodi Quar 
ries , as has been provided in the Standing 
Orders of the Cement Factory at Sawaimadho 

pur, is justified ? 
6 . Whether in view of the past Agreements , the 

demand of the Sangh for re -designation and 
regradation of workers of Quarries whose 
names are mentioned in Annexure III in accord 
ance with their jobs , is justified ? If so , to 

what relief are the workmen entitled ? 
7 . Whether Sick Leave with pay should be allowed 

to be accumulated upto 6 years instead of 3 
years as at present, in case of Operatives and 
5 years in the case of members of staff and 
whether half of the accumulated Sick -Leave 
with pay be permitted to be encashed or not , 

at the time of leaving of service ? 
8 . Whether any revision should be made in the 

existing rate of Stone -Cutters at the Quarries , 

if so, to what extent ? 
9 . Whether the existing scheme of Gratuity needs 

any revision , and if so , in what manner ? 
10 . Whether the existing provisions of Dust Allow 

ance , Equipment Allowance and Seasonal 
Uniforms be extended to new categories of 
employees as mentioned in Annexure IV keep 
ing in view the provisions of the Wage Board 

Report ? 
The Parties Agree that joint applications shall be 
filed before the Industrial Tribunal Rajasthan , Jaipur, 
with the request to pass " No Dispute Award " in the 
following cases :

___ I. फ्लोदी और राजरखो / भोरोंपुरा की लाइमस्टोन खदानों 
के विनिर्दिष्ट विवादग्रस्त विषय जिन पर मध्यस्थता की आवश्यकता 


का० प्रा० 572 . - - यत : मैसर्स जयपुर उद्योग लिमिटेड, 
सवाई माधोपुर के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्म 
कारों के बीच , जिनका प्रतिनिधित्व सीमेन्ट वर्कस कर्मचारी संघ , 
सवाई माधोपुर करती है, एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ; 

और यत : उक्त नियोजकों और कर्मकारों ने प्रीधोगिक 
विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 का 14 ) की धारा 10 - क 
की उपधारा ( 1 ) के उपबन्धों के अनुसरण में एक लिखित करार 
द्वारा उक्त विवाद को उसमें वणित पक्ति के माध्यस्थम् के लिए निदे 
शित करने का करार कर लिया है और उक्त माध्यस्थम् करार की 
एफ प्रप्ति केन्द्रीय सरकार को भेजी गई है । 

प्रतः, अब, प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 10 - क की उपधारा ( 3 ) के उपबन्धों के 
अनुसरण में , केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त माध्यस्थम् करार को , जो उसे 
17 जनवरी, 1972 को मिला था , एतद्वारा प्रकाशित करता 


1. क्या मैसर्स जयपुर उद्योग लिमिटेड, सवाई माधोपुर 

बारा लेखा वर्ष 1964 - 65 के लिए पहले ही 
4 . 73 प्रतिशत की दर से दिया गया बोनस , 
जैसा कि लाभ- हानि लेखा के तुलनपन के अन्तर्गत 
निकाला गया, बोनस भुगतान अधिनियम , 1965 
के अनुसार था या नहीं ? और क्या वे कर्मकार , 
जिनका प्रतिनिधित्व सीमेन्ट वर्क्स कर्मचारी संघ ,

निकाले गए पाबंटय अतिरेक के अनुसार लेखा 
वर्षों 1965 - 66, 1966 - 67 , 1967 - 68 और 
1968- 69 के लिए पहले ही क्रमश : 4 प्रतिशत , 
4 प्रतिशत 5 प्रतिशत पीर 4 प्रतिशत की दर से 
दिया गया बोनस , बोनस भूगतान अधिनियम ,1965 
के अनुसार है या नहीं ? और क्या वे कर्मकार जिनका 
प्रतिनिधित्व सीमेन्ट वर्क्स कर्मचारी संघ, सवाई 
माधोपुर करता है , विधान के अन्तर्गत प्रश्नगत

V . विवाद द्वारा प्रभावित या 
संभाव्यत : प्रभावित होने वाले 
कर्मकारों की प्राक्कलित संख्या 


पक्ष सहमत है कि औद्योगिक प्रधिकरण, राजस्थान , जयपुर के 
समक्ष संयुक्त रूप से पावेदन दायर किए जाएंगे जिनमें निम्नलिखित 
मामलों में कोई विवाद नहीं "पंचाट " जारी करने के लिए प्रार्थना 
की जाएगी : - -

मध्यस्थ अपन पंचाट छः मास की कालावधि या इतने और 
समय के भीतर जो हमारे बीच पारस्परिक लिखित करार मारा 
बढ़ावा जाय , देगा । यदि पूर्व वणित कालावधि के भीतर पंचाट 
नहीं दिया जाता तो मध्यस्थम् के लिए निर्देश स्वत : रद हो जायेगा 
पौर हम नये माध्यस्थम के लिए बातचीत करने को स्वतंत्र होंगे 
जिसके साक्ष्य में इस करार के पक्ष माज तारीख 22 दिसम्बर , 
1971 को सवाईमाधोपुर में साक्षियों की उपस्थिति में अपने 
हस्ताक्षर करते हैं :--- 


सोमन्ट बर्स कर्मचारी संघ के जयपुर उद्योग लिमिटेड का 
माध्यम से कर्मकारों का प्रति - प्रतिनिधित्व करने वाले 
निधित्व करने वाले 


हस्ताक्षर/ 
इन्दर राज शर्मा 
प्रधान 


हस्ताक्षर/ 
वेद लीखा 
वारष्ठ कमिक प्रबन्धक 


पक्ष और पागे सहमत है कि संघ द्वारा 13- 11-- 1969 को भेजे 
गए मांगों के चार्टर में सम्मिलित उन मांगों का , जिन्हें मध्यस्थ के 
पास निर्देशित नहीं किया गया है, पक्षों के बीच निपटारा हो चुका 
माना जाएगा और संघ या कर्मकार इन मामलों के बारे में और 
प्रागे कोई मांग या विवाद नहीं उठाएंगे । 
द्वितीय वंतन बोर्ड प्रचालन तक पक्ष प्रौद्योगिक सानांजस्य प्रार 
सामान्य उत्पादन बनाए रखने के लिए भी सहमत है और इस साया 
वधि के दौरान वे किसी प्रकार की एक पक्षीय कार्यवाही गुरू 
नहीं करेंगे जिससे काम की गति मन्द पड़े या किसी भी रूप में काम 
में जानबूझ कर रूकावट पड़े ।

ORDER 
New Delhi, the 17th December 1971 
S . O . 575 . — Whereas the Central Government is of 
opinion that an industrial dispute exists between the 
employers in relation to the management of Messrs 
J . M . Baxi and Company , Madras and their workntern in 
respect of the matters specified in the Schedule hereto 
annexed ; 

And , whereas , the Central Government considers it 
desirable to refer the said dispute for adjudication ; 

Now , therefore , in exercise of the powers conferred by 
Section 7A and clause ( d ) of sub- section ( 1 ) of section 
10 of the Industrial Disputes Act , 1947 ( 14 of 1947 ) , the 
Central Government hereby constitutes an Industrial 
Tribunal of which Shri K . Seetharama Rao shall be the 
Presiding Officer , with headquarters at Madras and 
refers the said dispute for adjudication to the said Tri 
bunal . 

SCIICDULU 
" Whether the workers cmployed by Messrs. J. M . 
Baxi and Company , Madras - 1 are entitled to a bonus 
of 8 - 1 / 3 per cent for the years 1969 - 70 and 1970 71 on 
the total earnings of the workers ? If not , what should 
be the rate of bonus payable to them? " 

[ No. L - 33011 / 12 / 71 - P & D. ] 

K . D. HAJELA, Dy Secy .

following rules further to amend the Indian Post 
Office Rules , 1933 , narmely : -- --

oince ( 5th Amendment ) Rules, 1972.. 

(2 ) They shall come into force on the 15th 
February , 1972.

to sub- rule ( 1 ) , the following proviso shall be added , 
namely :

का० प्रा० 577 -जांच आयोग अधिनियम 1952 ( 1952 
का 60 ) की धारा 3 द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते 
हए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतदद्वारा भारत सरकार के भूतपूर्व प्रौद्योगिक 
विकास प्रांतरिक व्यापार तथा समवाय - कार्य मंत्रालय ( प्रौद्योगिक 
विकास विभाग ) की अधिसूचना सं० का० प्रा० 711 दिनांक 18 
फरवरी, 1970 ( जैसा कि बाद में संशोधित किया गया ) में और 
आगे निम्नलिखित संशोधन करती है अर्थात : - - 


उक्त अधिसूचना के अन्तिम पैराग्राफ में " दो वर्ष " शब्दों के 
स्थान पर " तीन वर्ष " शब्द रखे जायेंगे ।

( 1898 का 6 ) की धारा 7 द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते

ELECTION COMMISSION OF INDIA 

New Delhi, the 1st February 1972 
S . O . 582. - In pursuance of clause (b ) of clause ( d ) 
of sub - section ( 2 ) of section 116 ( C ) of the Representa 
tion of the People Act, 1961, the Election Commission 
hereby publishes the Judgement of the Supreme Court 
of India , dated the 3rd January , 1972 in Clyil Appeal 
No. 1178- ( NCE) of 1971 against the Order dated the 
22nd July , 1971 of the High Court of Madhya Pradesh , 
Jabalpur, in Election Petition No . 6 of 1971, 

IN THE SUPREME COURT OF INDIA 
CIVIL APPELLATE JURISDICTION 

CIVIL APPEAL No. 1178 OF 1971 
Sri Chandrakant Shukla - Appellant 

Vs. 
Maharaja Martand Singh - Respondent 

JUDGMENT 
HEGDE. J . This appeal under section 116A of the 
Representation of People Act, 1951, is directed against 
the decision of the Madhya Pradesh High Court Dig 
missing the election petition filed by the appellant on 
the ground that the same is barred by limitatien. 

The election petitioner is a voter in the Rewa Parlla 
mentary constituency . The election with which we are 
concerned in this appeal was to the Parliament. That 
election was held on March 1, 1971 . The counting was 
over on March 10, 1971. The same day the election 
results were announced . The respondent was declared 
elected . The election results were published in the 
official gazette on March 15 , 1971 . The election petition 
was Aled on April 29 , 1971. 

Section 81( 1 ) of the Representation of People Act , 
1951, provides that an election petition calling in ques 
tion any election may be presented on one or more of 
the grounds specified in sub -section ( 1 ) of section 100 
and section 101 to the High Court by any candidate at 
such election or by any elector within forty - five days 
from , but not earllar than , the date of election of the 
returned candidate . or if there are more than one 
returned candidate at the election and the dates of their 
election are different, the later of those two dates. 
Section 67A says : 
" For the purposes of this Act, the date on which a 

candidate is declared by returning officer under 
the provisions of section 53 or section 66 , to be 
elected to a House of Parliament or of the 
Legislature of a State shall be the date of elec 

tion of that candidate. " 
Reading these two sections together it is clear that tho 
election petition should have been Aled within torty 
Ave days from March 10 , 1971. Hence , the petition filed 
by the appellant is clearly barred by limitation.

भारत निर्वाचन आयोग 


Through it was contended before the High Court that 
the election petition was Aled within time, that conten 
tion was not pressed before us. The only contention 
taken before us was that there was suficient cause to 
condone the delay in Alling the election petition . As we 
are unable to accept that contention we are not examin 
ing the question whether the High Court had any 
power to condone the delay in Alling the election peti 
tion . 

Now coming to the question whether there was suffl 
cient cause for condoning the delay the High Court held 
that no such sufficient cause was shown . This is essen 
tially a finding of fact and this court ordinarily does 
not interfere with the decision of the High Court on 
questions of fact . Further, the relief asked for is essen . 
tially a discretionary relief and when a trial court exer 
cises its discretion an appellate court is reluctant to 
interfere with that discretion unless there are very good 
grounds for doing so . 

Now , turning to the facts of this case , in the applica 
tion filed for condonation of delay only reason advanced 
is that the pititioner believes that he has filed his elec 
tlon petition within time but if the court takes a 
different view then the delay may be condoned . No 
cause was shown for condoning the delay. But while 
giving evidence in the court the petitioner went much 
further. He deposed that before flling the election 
petition be consulted Shri Hari Shankar Saxena , 
an advocate, and that advocate told him that 
he has to Ale the election petitin within forty 
Ave days of the publication of the result of the 
election in the gazette . No such vehement was 
made in the petition wherein he sought the con 
donation of the delay nor Shri Hari Shankar Saxena 
was examined as witness in the case. Under those cir 
cumstances the High Court rightly disbelived the evid . 
ence of the petitioner to the effect that he had consulted 
Shri Hari Shankar Şaxena before filling the election 
petition . The High Court was of the opinion that the 
petitioner s version that he consulted Shri Hari Shankar 
Saxena is an after - thought. We agree with that con 
clusion . The petitioner has given no satisfactory ex 
planation for the delay in fling the election petition . 
Hence his election petition was rightly dismissed by the 
High Court . 

In the result this appeal falls and the same is dis 
missed with costs . 

J. 
K . S . Hegde,

ORDER 
Bombay , the 3rd February 1972 
$ . 0 , 584 . — In pursuance of note ( 3 ) of the Schedule 
to the Notification of the Government of India in the 
Ministry of Shipping & Transport relating to scales of 
provision for seamen , No. S .O . 2169 , dated 218t June, 
1987, and in furtherance of the order of Director Gene 
ral of Shipping No . 9 (21) - CRA / 67, dated 3rd February , 
1972 , I, R . Doraiswamy, Director General of Shipping , 
being satisfied that rice supply position in the country 
has improved , hereby order that with etfect from the 
date of this order the total daily scale of cereal rations 
of 570 grams shall be restored to 400 gramg rice and 
170 grams wheat, if procurement is made in India , 

[No. 9 (21)-CRA / 07.) 

R DORAISWAMY, 
Director General of Shipping.
